====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If death results from an accident at any time, what benefit is paid?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The full benefit amount ($10,000) is paid immediately, plus all premiums paid.
EVIDENCE: EXCEPTION - ACCIDENTAL DEATH: If death results from an accident at any time, we pay the full benefit amount immediately, plus all premiums paid.
====================================================================================================

rewritten_query: What benefit is paid when death occurs due to an accident?
reasoning: The query asks about the benefit paid when death results from an accident at any time. This is a specific question about insurance or social benefits, likely related to life insurance, accidental death and dismemberment (AD&D) insurance, or government-provided benefits (e.g., workers' compensation or social security). The key here is identifying the type of benefit paid under such circumstances.

The query does not mention any specific person, organization, or policy, so it's asking for a general rule or benefit type. Therefore, it's a single-fact query: identifying the benefit paid in the event of death due to an accident.

No chaining of facts is needed—there’s no dependency on a prior lookup. The answer can be directly determined by knowing the standard benefit associated with accidental death.
